Convert to string\n    value_str = str(value).strip()\n\n    # Empty string check\n    if not value_str or value_str == '':\n        return None\n\n    # Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n    value_str = value_str.replace(',', '.')\n\n    # Try to convert to float\n    try:\n        return float(value_str)\n    except (ValueError, TypeError):\n        return None\n\n\ndef detect_and_convert_unit(value, feature_name, min_val, max_val, conversion_factor):\n    \"\"\"\n    Detect if value is in alternative unit and convert if needed.\n\n    For many tests, SI units produce values that are much higher or lower than US conventional units.\n    - If value is outside expected range, apply conversion\n    - Conversion factors are designed to bring SI values into US range\n    \"\"\"\n    if value is None or pd.isna(value):\n        return value\n\n    # Special handling for different features\n\n    # Creatinine: µmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 44-885 µmol/L, US range: 0.5-10 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = µmol/L / 88.4\n    if feature_name == 'Serum_Creatinine':\n        if value > 15.0:  # Likely in µmol/L\n            return value / 88.4\n\n    # BUN: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 2.1-7.1 mmol/L, US range: 6-20 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 2.8\n    elif feature_name == 'BUN':\n        if value < 6.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 2.8\n\n    # Hemoglobin: g/L (SI) -> g/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 120-180 g/L, US range: 12-18 g/dL\n    # Conversion: g/dL = g/L / 10\n    elif feature_name == 'Hemoglobin':\n        if value > 25.0:  # Likely in g/L\n            return value / 10.0\n\n    # Serum Calcium: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 2.2-2.6 mmol/L, US range: 8.5-10.5 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 4.0\n    elif feature_name == 'Serum_Calcium':\n        if value < 4.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 4.0\n\n    # Ionized Calcium: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 1.1-1.4 mmol/L, US range: 4.4-5.6 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 4.0\n    # BUT: Many labs report ionized calcium in mg/dL already (range 4-6)\n    elif feature_name == 'Ionized_Calcium':\n        if value < 2.5:  # Likely in mmol/L (SI units typically 1.1-1.4)\n            return value * 4.0\n        # If already in range 3-8, it's likely already mg/dL\n\n    # Phosphorus: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 0.8-1.5 mmol/L, US range: 2.5-4.5 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 3.1\n    elif feature_name == 'Phosphorus':\n        if value < 2.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 3.1\n\n    # Magnesium: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 0.7-1.0 mmol/L, US range: 1.7-2.4 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 2.43\n    elif feature_name == 'Magnesium':\n        if value < 1.5:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 2.43\n\n    # Bilirubin: µmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 5-20 µmol/L, US range: 0.3-1.2 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = µmol/L * 0.0585\n    elif feature_name in ['Total_Bilirubin', 'Direct_Bilirubin']:\n        if value > 50.0:  # Likely in µmol/L\n            return value * 0.0585\n\n    # Albumin/Total Protein: g/L (SI) -> g/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 35-50 g/L, US range: 3.5-5.0 g/dL\n    # Conversion: g/dL = g/L / 10\n    elif feature_name in ['Albumin_Serum', 'Total_Protein']:\n        if value > 10.0:  # Likely in g/L\n            return value / 10.0\n\n    # Glucose: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 3.9-6.1 mmol/L, US range: 70-110 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 18\n    elif feature_name == 'Glucose':\n        if value < 50.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 18.0\n\n    # Uric Acid: µmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 150-420 µmol/L, US range: 2.5-7.0 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = µmol/L / 59.48\n    elif feature_name == 'Uric_Acid':\n        if value > 25.0:  # Likely in µmol/L\n            return value / 59.48\n\n    # Cholesterol and lipids: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # Total cholesterol SI: 3.9-5.2 mmol/L, US: 150-200 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 38.67\n    elif feature_name in ['Total_Cholesterol', 'LDL_Cholesterol', 'Non_HDL_Cholesterol']:\n        if value < 50.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 38.67\n    elif feature_name == 'HDL_Cholesterol':\n        if value < 10.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 38.67\n\n    # Triglycerides: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 0.4-1.8 mmol/L, US range: 35-160 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 88.57\n    elif feature_name == 'Triglycerides':\n        if value < 20.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 88.57\n\n    # Iron: µmol/L (SI) -> µg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 9-30 µmol/L, US range: 50-170 µg/dL\n    # Conversion: µg/dL = µmol/L * 5.587\n    elif feature_name in ['Serum_Iron', 'TIBC']:\n        if value < 50.0:  # Likely in µmol/L\n            return value * 5.587\n\n    # Lactate: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 0.5-2.2 mmol/L, US range: 4.5-20 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 9.0\n    # Only convert if very small (< 3.0), as some mg/dL values can be in 4-20 range\n    elif feature_name == 'Lactate':\n        if value < 3.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 9.0\n\n    # CRP: mg/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # Some labs report in mg/L, others in mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mg/L / 10\n    elif feature_name == 'CRP':\n        if value > 100.0:  # Likely in mg/L\n            return value / 10.0\n\n    # Free T4: pmol/L (SI) -> ng/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 10-25 pmol/L, US range: 0.8-2.0 ng/dL\n    # Conversion: ng/dL = pmol/L * 0.0777\n    elif feature_name == 'Free_T4':\n        if value > 10.0:  # Likely in pmol/L\n            return value * 0.0777\n\n    # Free T3: pmol/L (SI) -> pg/mL (US)\n    # SI range: 3.5-6.5 pmol/L, US range: 2.3-4.2 pg/mL\n    # Conversion: pg/mL = pmol/L * 0.65\n    elif feature_name == 'Free_T3':\n        if value > 10.0:  # Likely in pmol/L\n            return value * 0.65\n\n    return value\n\n\ndef harmonize_lab_data(input_file, output_file):\n    \"\"\"\n    Main function to harmonize clinical lab data.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Reading data from {input_file}...\")\n    df = pd.read_csv(input_file, dtype=str)\n\n    print(f\"Original dataset: {len(df)} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    # Step 1: Clean all numeric values (handle scientific notation and comma decimals)\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Cleaning numeric values (scientific notation, commas)...\")\n    lab_features = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    for col in lab_features:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(clean_numeric_value)\n\n    # Step 2: Remove rows with missing values\n    print(\"\\nStep 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df_clean = df.dropna().copy()  # Use .copy() to avoid SettingWithCopyWarning\n    rows_after = len(df_clean)\n    print(f\"  Removed {rows_before - rows_after} rows with missing values\")\n    print(f\"  Remaining: {rows_after} rows\")\n\n    # Step 3: Unit conversion for each feature\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Converting mixed units to US conventional units...\")\n    conversion_count = {}\n\n    for feature in lab_features:\n        if feature in UNIT_CONVERSIONS:\n            min_val, max_val, conv_factor, si_unit, us_unit = UNIT_CONVERSIONS[feature]\n\n            # Track conversions\n            original_values = df_clean[feature].copy()\n            df_clean[feature] = df_clean[feature].apply(\n                lambda x: detect_and_convert_unit(x, feature, min_val, max_val, conv_factor)\n            )\n\n            # Convert to string\n    value_str = str(value).strip()\n\n    # Empty string check\n    if not value_str or value_str == '':\n        return None\n\n    # Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n    value_str = value_str.replace(',', '.')\n\n    # Try to convert to float\n    try:\n        return float(value_str)\n    except (ValueError, TypeError):\n        return None\n\n\ndef detect_and_convert_unit(value, feature_name, min_val, max_val, conversion_factor):\n    \"\"\"\n    Detect if value is in alternative unit and convert if needed.\n\n    For many tests, SI units produce values that are much higher or lower than US conventional units.\n    - If value is outside expected range, apply conversion\n    - Conversion factors are designed to bring SI values into US range\n    \"\"\"\n    if value is None or pd.isna(value):\n        return value\n\n    # Likely in µmol/L\n            return value / 88.4\n\n    # BUN: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 2.1-7.1 mmol/L, US range: 6-20 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 2.8\n    elif feature_name == 'BUN':\n        if value < 6.0:  # Likely in g/L\n            return value / 10.0\n\n    # Serum Calcium: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 2.2-2.6 mmol/L, US range: 8.5-10.5 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 4.0\n    elif feature_name == 'Serum_Calcium':\n        if value < 4.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 4.0\n\n    # Ionized Calcium: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 1.1-1.4 mmol/L, US range: 4.4-5.6 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 4.0\n    # BUT: Many labs report ionized calcium in mg/dL already (range 4-6)\n    elif feature_name == 'Ionized_Calcium':\n        if value < 2.5:  # Likely in mmol/L (SI units typically 1.1-1.4)\n            return value * 4.0\n        # If already in range 3-8, it's likely already mg/dL\n\n    # Phosphorus: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 0.8-1.5 mmol/L, US range: 2.5-4.5 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 3.1\n    elif feature_name == 'Phosphorus':\n        if value < 2.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 3.1\n\n    # Magnesium: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 0.7-1.0 mmol/L, US range: 1.7-2.4 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 2.43\n    elif feature_name == 'Magnesium':\n        if value < 1.5:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 2.43\n\n    # Bilirubin: µmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 5-20 µmol/L, US range: 0.3-1.2 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = µmol/L * 0.0585\n    elif feature_name in ['Total_Bilirubin', 'Direct_Bilirubin']:\n        if value > 50.0:  # Likely in µmol/L\n            return value * 0.0585\n\n    # Albumin/Total Protein: g/L (SI) -> g/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 35-50 g/L, US range: 3.5-5.0 g/dL\n    # Conversion: g/dL = g/L / 10\n    elif feature_name in ['Albumin_Serum', 'Total_Protein']:\n        if value > 10.0:  # Likely in g/L\n            return value / 10.0\n\n    # Glucose: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 3.9-6.1 mmol/L, US range: 70-110 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 18\n    elif feature_name == 'Glucose':\n        if value < 50.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 18.0\n\n    # Uric Acid: µmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 150-420 µmol/L, US range: 2.5-7.0 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = µmol/L / 59.48\n    elif feature_name == 'Uric_Acid':\n        if value > 25.0:  # Likely in µmol/L\n            return value / 59.48\n\n    # Cholesterol and lipids: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # Total cholesterol SI: 3.9-5.2 mmol/L, US: 150-200 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 38.67\n    elif feature_name in ['Total_Cholesterol', 'LDL_Cholesterol', 'Non_HDL_Cholesterol']:\n        if value < 50.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 38.67\n    elif feature_name == 'HDL_Cholesterol':\n        if value < 10.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 38.67\n\n    # Triglycerides: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 0.4-1.8 mmol/L, US range: 35-160 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 88.57\n    elif feature_name == 'Triglycerides':\n        if value < 20.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 88.57\n\n    # Iron: µmol/L (SI) -> µg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 9-30 µmol/L, US range: 50-170 µg/dL\n    # Conversion: µg/dL = µmol/L * 5.587\n    elif feature_name in ['Serum_Iron', 'TIBC']:\n        if value < 50.0:  # Likely in µmol/L\n            return value * 5.587\n\n    # Lactate: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 0.5-2.2 mmol/L, US range: 4.5-20 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 9.0\n    # Only convert if very small (< 3.0), as some mg/dL values can be in 4-20 range\n    elif feature_name == 'Lactate':\n        if value < 3.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 9.0\n\n    # CRP: mg/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # Some labs report in mg/L, others in mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mg/L / 10\n    elif feature_name == 'CRP':\n        if value > 100.0:  # Likely in mg/L\n            return value / 10.0\n\n    # Free T4: pmol/L (SI) -> ng/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 10-25 pmol/L, US range: 0.8-2.0 ng/dL\n    # Conversion: ng/dL = pmol/L * 0.0777\n    elif feature_name == 'Free_T4':\n        if value > 10.0:  # Likely in pmol/L\n            return value * 0.0777\n\n    # Free T3: pmol/L (SI) -> pg/mL (US)\n    # SI range: 3.5-6.5 pmol/L, US range: 2.3-4.2 pg/mL\n    # Conversion: pg/mL = pmol/L * 0.65\n    elif feature_name == 'Free_T3':\n        if value > 10.0:  # Likely in pmol/L\n            return value * 0.65\n\n    return value\n\n\ndef harmonize_lab_data(input_file, output_file):\n    \"\"\"\n    Main function to harmonize clinical lab data.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Reading data from {input_file}...\")\n    df = pd.read_csv(input_file, dtype=str)\n\n    print(f\"Original dataset: {len(df)} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    # Step 1: Clean all numeric values (handle scientific notation and comma decimals)\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Cleaning numeric values (scientific notation, commas)...\")\n    lab_features = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    for col in lab_features:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(clean_numeric_value)\n\n    # Step 2: Remove rows with missing values\n    print(\"\\nStep 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df_clean = df.dropna().copy()  # Use .copy() to avoid SettingWithCopyWarning\n    rows_after = len(df_clean)\n    print(f\"  Removed {rows_before - rows_after} rows with missing values\")\n    print(f\"  Remaining: {rows_after} rows\")\n\n    # Try to convert to float\n    try:\n        return float(value_str)\n    except (ValueError, TypeError):\n        return None\n\n\ndef detect_and_convert_unit(value, feature_name, min_val, max_val, conversion_factor):\n    \"\"\"\n    Detect if value is in alternative unit and convert if needed.\n\n    For many tests, SI units produce values that are much higher or lower than US conventional units.\n    - If value is outside expected range, apply conversion\n    - Conversion factors are designed to bring SI values into US range\n    \"\"\"\n    if value is None or pd.isna(value):\n        return value\n\n    # Likely in µmol/L\n            return value / 88.4\n\n    # BUN: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 2.1-7.1 mmol/L, US range: 6-20 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 2.8\n    elif feature_name == 'BUN':\n        if value < 6.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 4.0\n\n    # Ionized Calcium: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 1.1-1.4 mmol/L, US range: 4.4-5.6 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 4.0\n    # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 2.43\n\n    # Bilirubin: µmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 5-20 µmol/L, US range: 0.3-1.2 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = µmol/L * 0.0585\n    elif feature_name in ['Total_Bilirubin', 'Direct_Bilirubin']:\n        if value > 50.0:  # Likely in µmol/L\n            return value * 0.0585\n\n    # Albumin/Total Protein: g/L (SI) -> g/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 35-50 g/L, US range: 3.5-5.0 g/dL\n    # Conversion: g/dL = g/L / 10\n    elif feature_name in ['Albumin_Serum', 'Total_Protein']:\n        if value > 10.0:  # Likely in g/L\n            return value / 10.0\n\n    # Glucose: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 3.9-6.1 mmol/L, US range: 70-110 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 18\n    elif feature_name == 'Glucose':\n        if value < 50.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 18.0\n\n    # Uric Acid: µmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 150-420 µmol/L, US range: 2.5-7.0 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = µmol/L / 59.48\n    elif feature_name == 'Uric_Acid':\n        if value > 25.0:  # Likely in µmol/L\n            return value / 59.48\n\n    # Cholesterol and lipids: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # Total cholesterol SI: 3.9-5.2 mmol/L, US: 150-200 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 38.67\n    elif feature_name in ['Total_Cholesterol', 'LDL_Cholesterol', 'Non_HDL_Cholesterol']:\n        if value < 50.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 38.67\n    elif feature_name == 'HDL_Cholesterol':\n        if value < 10.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 38.67\n\n    # Triglycerides: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 0.4-1.8 mmol/L, US range: 35-160 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 88.57\n    elif feature_name == 'Triglycerides':\n        if value < 20.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 88.57\n\n    # Iron: µmol/L (SI) -> µg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 9-30 µmol/L, US range: 50-170 µg/dL\n    # Conversion: µg/dL = µmol/L * 5.587\n    elif feature_name in ['Serum_Iron', 'TIBC']:\n        if value < 50.0:  # Likely in µmol/L\n            return value * 5.587\n\n    # Lactate: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 0.5-2.2 mmol/L, US range: 4.5-20 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 9.0\n    # Only convert if very small (< 3.0), as some mg/dL values can be in 4-20 range\n    elif feature_name == 'Lactate':\n        if value < 3.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 9.0\n\n    # CRP: mg/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # Some labs report in mg/L, others in mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mg/L / 10\n    elif feature_name == 'CRP':\n        if value > 100.0:  # Likely in mg/L\n            return value / 10.0\n\n    # Free T4: pmol/L (SI) -> ng/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 10-25 pmol/L, US range: 0.8-2.0 ng/dL\n    # Conversion: ng/dL = pmol/L * 0.0777\n    elif feature_name == 'Free_T4':\n        if value > 10.0:  # Likely in pmol/L\n            return value * 0.0777\n\n    # Free T3: pmol/L (SI) -> pg/mL (US)\n    # SI range: 3.5-6.5 pmol/L, US range: 2.3-4.2 pg/mL\n    # Conversion: pg/mL = pmol/L * 0.65\n    elif feature_name == 'Free_T3':\n        if value > 10.0:  # Likely in pmol/L\n            return value * 0.65\n\n    return value\n\n\ndef harmonize_lab_data(input_file, output_file):\n    \"\"\"\n    Main function to harmonize clinical lab data.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Reading data from {input_file}...\")\n    df = pd.read_csv(input_file, dtype=str)\n\n    print(f\"Original dataset: {len(df)} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    # Step 1: Clean all numeric values (handle scientific notation and comma decimals)\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Cleaning numeric values (scientific notation, commas)...\")\n    lab_features = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    for col in lab_features:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(clean_numeric_value)\n\n    # Step 2: Remove rows with missing values\n    print(\"\\nStep 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df_clean = df.dropna().copy()  # Use .copy() to avoid SettingWithCopyWarning\n    rows_after = len(df_clean)\n    print(f\"  Removed {rows_before - rows_after} rows with missing values\")\n    print(f\"  Remaining: {rows_after} rows\")\n\n    # Same unit\n}\n\n\ndef clean_numeric_value(value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Clean and convert a single value to float.\n    Handles: scientific notation, comma as decimal separator, whitespace\n    Returns None if value is missing or cannot be converted.\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return None\n\n    # Convert to string\n    value_str = str(value).strip()\n\n    # Empty string check\n    if not value_str or value_str == '':\n        return None\n\n    # Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n    value_str = value_str.replace(',', '.')\n\n    # Try to convert to float\n    try:\n        return float(value_str)\n    except (ValueError, TypeError):\n        return None\n\n\ndef detect_and_convert_unit(value, feature_name, min_val, max_val, conversion_factor):\n    \"\"\"\n    Detect if value is in alternative unit and convert if needed.\n\n    For many tests, SI units produce values that are much higher or lower than US conventional units.\n    - If value is outside expected range, apply conversion\n    - Conversion factors are designed to bring SI values into US range\n    \"\"\"\n    if value is None or pd.isna(value):\n        return value\n\n    # Special handling for different features\n\n    # Creatinine: µmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 44-885 µmol/L, US range: 0.5-10 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = µmol/L / 88.4\n    if feature_name == 'Serum_Creatinine':\n        if value > 15.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 2.8\n\n    # Hemoglobin: g/L (SI) -> g/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 120-180 g/L, US range: 12-18 g/dL\n    # Conversion: g/dL = g/L / 10\n    elif feature_name == 'Hemoglobin':\n        if value > 25.0:  # Likely in g/L\n            return value / 10.0\n\n    # Serum Calcium: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 2.2-2.6 mmol/L, US range: 8.5-10.5 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 4.0\n    elif feature_name == 'Serum_Calcium':\n        if value < 4.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 4.0\n\n    # Ionized Calcium: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 1.1-1.4 mmol/L, US range: 4.4-5.6 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 4.0\n    # BUT: Many labs report ionized calcium in mg/dL already (range 4-6)\n    elif feature_name == 'Ionized_Calcium':\n        if value < 2.5:  # Likely in mmol/L (SI units typically 1.1-1.4)\n            return value * 4.0\n        # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 2.43\n\n    # Bilirubin: µmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 5-20 µmol/L, US range: 0.3-1.2 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = µmol/L * 0.0585\n    elif feature_name in ['Total_Bilirubin', 'Direct_Bilirubin']:\n        if value > 50.0:  # Likely in µmol/L\n            return value * 0.0585\n\n    # Albumin/Total Protein: g/L (SI) -> g/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 35-50 g/L, US range: 3.5-5.0 g/dL\n    # Conversion: g/dL = g/L / 10\n    elif feature_name in ['Albumin_Serum', 'Total_Protein']:\n        if value > 10.0:  # Likely in g/L\n            return value / 10.0\n\n    # Glucose: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 3.9-6.1 mmol/L, US range: 70-110 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 18\n    elif feature_name == 'Glucose':\n        if value < 50.0:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value * 18.0\n\n    # Uric Acid: µmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 150-420 µmol/L, US range: 2.5-7.0 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = µmol/L / 59.48\n    elif feature_name == 'Uric_Acid':\n        if value > 25.0:  # Likely in µmol/L\n            return value / 59.48\n\n    # Cholesterol and lipids: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # Total cholesterol SI: 3.9-5.2 mmol/L, US: 150-200 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 38.67\n    elif feature_name in ['Total_Cholesterol', 'LDL_Cholesterol', 'Non_HDL_Cholesterol']:\n        if value < 50.0:  # Likely in µmol/L\n            return value * 5.587\n\n    # Lactate: mmol/L (SI) -> mg/dL (US)\n    # SI range: 0.5-2.2 mmol/L, US range: 4.5-20 mg/dL\n    # Conversion: mg/dL = mmol/L * 9.0\n    # Only convert if very small (< 3.0), as some mg/dL values can be in 4-20 range\n    elif feature_name == 'Lactate':\n        if value < 3.0:  # Likely in pmol/L\n            return value * 0.0777\n\n    # Free T3: pmol/L (SI) -> pg/mL (US)\n    # SI range: 3.5-6.5 pmol/L, US range: 2.3-4.2 pg/mL\n    # Conversion: pg/mL = pmol/L * 0.65\n    elif feature_name == 'Free_T3':\n        if value > 10.0:  # Likely in pmol/L\n            return value * 0.65\n\n    return value\n\n\ndef harmonize_lab_data(input_file, output_file):\n    \"\"\"\n    Main function to harmonize clinical lab data.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Reading data from {input_file}...\")\n    df = pd.read_csv(input_file, dtype=str)\n\n    print(f\"Original dataset: {len(df)} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    # Track conversions\n            original_values = df_clean[feature].copy()\n            df_clean[feature] = df_clean[feature].apply(\n                lambda x: detect_and_convert_unit(x, feature, min_val, max_val, conv_factor)\n            )\n\n            # Count how many values were converted\n            converted = sum(original_values != df_clean[feature])\n            if converted > 0:\n                conversion_count[feature] = converted\n\n    print(f\"\\n  Converted values in {len(conversion_count)} features:\")\n    for feature, count in sorted(conversion_count.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:10]:\n        print(f\"    {feature}: {count} values\")\n\n    # Format to ensure X.XX format\n        df_clean[col] = df_clean[col].apply(lambda x: f\"{x:.2f}\" if pd.notna(x) else x)\n\n    # Step 5: Validation - check for values outside physiological ranges\n    print(\"\\nStep 5: Validating physiological ranges...\")\n    out_of_range_features = {}\n    for feature in lab_features:\n        if feature in UNIT_CONVERSIONS:\n            min_val, max_val, _, _, _ = UNIT_CONVERSIONS[feature]\n            # Convert string back to float for validation\n            values = pd.to_numeric(df_clean[feature], errors='coerce')\n            out_of_range = sum((values < min_val) | (values > max_val))\n            if out_of_range > 0:\n                out_of_range_features[feature] = out_of_range\n\n    if out_of_range_features:\n        print(f\"\\n  Warning: {len(out_of_range_features)} features have some values outside expected ranges:\")\n        for feature, count in sorted(out_of_range_features.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:5]:\n            min_val, max_val, _, _, _ = UNIT_CONVERSIONS[feature]\n            print(f\"    {feature}: {count} values outside [{min_val}, {max_val}]\")\n    else:\n        print(\"  All values within expected physiological ranges!\")\n\n    #
